# EU law in Romanian: regulations and directives in force (Official Journal text): 2017

740 acts, 740 versions. Each act lives in a folder named by its official identifier, `<kind>/<year>/<number>/` (Legea nr. 31/1990 is `lege/1990/31/`); its README.md lists its articles and the decisions made by hand. Dates are in-force days. Generated on every publish; the same list is in [2017.json](2017.json).
